2247  8996              ; Game engine code --------------------------------------------------------------
2248  8996
2249  8996              ; Arcade Game Designer.
2250  8996              ; (C) 2008 - 2020 Jonathan Cauldwell.
2251  8996              ; ZX Spectrum Engine v0.7.10
2252  8996
2253  8996              ; Global definitions.
2254  8996
2255  8996              SIMASK equ 248             ; SPRITEINK mask - allows users to set BRIGHT/FLASH/CLUT as well.
2256  8996              SHRAPN equ 63926           ; shrapnel table, just below screen address table.
2257  8996              SCADTB equ 64256           ; screen address table, just below map.
2258  8996              MAP    equ 64768           ; properties map buffer.
2259  8996              loopa  equ 23681           ; loop counter system variable.
2260  8996              loopb  equ 23728           ; loop counter system variable.
2261  8996              loopc  equ 23729           ; loop counter system variable.
2262  8996
2263  8996              ; Block characteristics.
2264  8996
2265  8996              PLATFM equ 1               ; platform.
2266  8996              WALL   equ PLATFM + 1      ; solid wall.
2267  8996              LADDER equ WALL + 1        ; ladder.
2268  8996              FODDER equ LADDER + 1      ; fodder block.
2269  8996              DEADLY equ FODDER + 1      ; deadly block.
2270  8996              CUSTOM equ DEADLY + 1      ; custom block.
2271  8996              WATER  equ CUSTOM + 1      ; water block.
2272  8996              COLECT equ WATER + 1       ; collectable block.
2273  8996              NUMTYP equ COLECT + 1      ; number of types.
2274  8996
2275  8996              ; Sprites.
2276  8996
2277  8996              NUMSPR equ 19              ; number of sprites.
2278  8996              TABSIZ equ 17              ; size of each entry.
2279  8996              SPRBUF equ NUMSPR * TABSIZ ; size of entire table.
2280  8996              NMESIZ equ 4               ; bytes stored in nmetab for each sprite.
2281  8996              X      equ 8               ; new x coordinate of sprite.
2282  8996              Y      equ X + 1           ; new y coordinate of sprite.
2283  8996              PAM1ST equ 5               ; first sprite parameter, old x (ix+5).
2284  8996
2285  8996              ; Particle engine.
2286  8996
2287  8996              NUMSHR equ 55              ; pieces of shrapnel.
2288  8996              SHRSIZ equ 6               ; bytes per particle.
2289  8996
2290  8996
2291  8996              ; Game starts here.
2292  8996              ; Musicizer relies on distance between start and varm remaining constant at 34 bytes.
2293  8996              ; Do not mess with this section or users will be unable to control music with the M variable.
2294  8996
2295  8996              start  equ $
2296  8996
2297  8996              ; Set up the font.
2298  8996
2299  8996 21 8A 85            ld hl,font-256      ; address of font.
2300  8999 22 36 5C            ld (23606),hl       ; set up game font.
2301  899C
2302  899C              ; if you wish to exit to BASIC at the end of each game, change "jp gamelp" to "jp game".
2303  899C C3 96 90            jp gamelp           ; start the game.
2304  899F
2305  899F 00           joyval defb 0              ; joystick reading.
2306  89A0 00           frmno  defb 0              ; selected frame.
2307  89A1
2308  89A1              ; Don't change the order of these four.  Menu routine relies on winlft following wintop.
2309  89A1
2310  89A1 08           wintop defb WINDOWTOP      ; top of window.
2311  89A2 01           winlft defb WINDOWLFT      ; left edge.
2312  89A3 0B           winhgt defb WINDOWHGT      ; window height.
2313  89A4 1E           winwid defb WINDOWWID      ; window width.
2314  89A5
2315  89A5 01           numob  defb NUMOBJ         ; number of objects in game.
2316  89A6
2317  89A6              ; Variables start here.
2318  89A6              ; Pixel versions of wintop, winlft, winhgt, winwid.
2319  89A6
2320  89A6 40           wntopx defb (8 * WINDOWTOP)
2321  89A7 08           wnlftx defb (8 * WINDOWLFT)
2322  89A8 88           wnbotx defb ((WINDOWTOP * 8) + (WINDOWHGT * 8) - 16)
2323  89A9 E8           wnrgtx defb ((WINDOWLFT * 8) + (WINDOWWID * 8) - 16)
2324  89AA 00           scno   defb 0              ; present screen number.
2325  89AB 03           numlif defb 3              ; number of lives.
2326  89AC 00           vara   defb 0              ; general-purpose variable.
2327  89AD 00           varb   defb 0              ; general-purpose variable.
2328  89AE 00           varc   defb 0              ; general-purpose variable.
2329  89AF 00           vard   defb 0              ; general-purpose variable.
2330  89B0 00           vare   defb 0              ; general-purpose variable.
2331  89B1 00           varf   defb 0              ; general-purpose variable.
2332  89B2 00           varg   defb 0              ; general-purpose variable.
2333  89B3 00           varh   defb 0              ; general-purpose variable.
2334  89B4 00           vari   defb 0              ; general-purpose variable.
2335  89B5 00           varj   defb 0              ; general-purpose variable.
2336  89B6 00           vark   defb 0              ; general-purpose variable.
2337  89B7 00           varl   defb 0              ; general-purpose variable.
2338  89B8 00           varm   defb 0              ; general-purpose variable.
2339  89B9 00           varn   defb 0              ; general-purpose variable.
2340  89BA 00           varo   defb 0              ; general-purpose variable.
2341  89BB 00           varp   defb 0              ; general-purpose variable.
2342  89BC 00           varq   defb 0              ; general-purpose variable.
2343  89BD 00           varr   defb 0              ; general-purpose variable.
2344  89BE 00           vars   defb 0              ; general-purpose variable.
2345  89BF 00           vart   defb 0              ; general-purpose variable.
2346  89C0 00           varu   defb 0              ; general-purpose variable.
2347  89C1 00           varv   defb 0              ; general-purpose variable.
2348  89C2 00           varw   defb 0              ; general-purpose variable.
2349  89C3 00           varz   defb 0              ; general-purpose variable.
2350  89C4 00           contrl defb 0              ; control, 0 = keyboard, 1 = Kempston, 2 = Sinclair, 3 = Mouse.
2351  89C5 00           charx  defb 0              ; cursor x position.
2352  89C6 00           chary  defb 0              ; cursor y position.
2353  89C7 00           clock  defb 0              ; last clock reading.
2354  89C8 FF           varrnd defb 255            ; last random number.
2355  89C9 FE           varobj defb 254            ; last object number.
2356  89CA FF           varopt defb 255            ; last option chosen from menu.
2357  89CB FF           varblk defb 255            ; block type.
2358  89CC 00           nexlev defb 0              ; next level flag.
2359  89CD 00           restfl defb 0              ; restart screen flag.
2360  89CE 00           deadf  defb 0              ; dead flag.
2361  89CF 00           gamwon defb 0              ; game won flag.
2362  89D0 00           dispx  defb 0              ; cursor x position.
2363  89D1 00           dispy  defb 0              ; cursor y position.
2364  89D2
2365  89D2              ; Make sure pointers are arranged in the same order as the data itself.
2366  89D2
2367  89D2 88 85        frmptr defw frmlst         ; sprite frames.
2368  89D4 36 74        blkptr defw chgfx          ; block graphics.
2369  89D6 DE 74        colptr defw bcol           ; address of char colours.
2370  89D8 F3 74        proptr defw bprop          ; address of char properties.
2371  89DA F7 72        scrptr defw scdat          ; address of screens.
2372  89DC AC 85        nmeptr defw nmedat         ; enemy start positions.
2373  89DE
2374  89DE              ; Assorted game routines which can go in contended memory.
2375  89DE
2376  89DE              ; Modify for inventory.
2377  89DE
2378  89DE 21 5E 8B     minve  ld hl,invdis        ; routine address.
2379  89E1 22 69 8A            ld (mod0+1),hl      ; set up menu routine.
2380  89E4 22 0F 8A            ld (mod2+1),hl      ; set up count routine.
2381  89E7 21 6B 8B            ld hl,fopt          ; find option from available objects.
2382  89EA 22 F1 8A            ld (mod1+1),hl      ; set up routine.
2383  89ED 18 0F               jr dbox             ; do menu routine.

2828  8CEF
2829  8CEF              ; Set palette routine and data.
2830  8CEF              ; Palette.
2831  8CEF              ; 48955 = register select port.
2832  8CEF              ; 65339 = data read/write port.
2833  8CEF
2834  8CEF              ; 48955 = write to register:
2835  8CEF              ; d0-d5 : select register sub-group.
2836  8CEF              ; d6-d7 : select register group.
2837  8CEF              ;         00 = sub-group determines entry in palette table.
2838  8CEF              ;         64 = mode select, write d0 to 65339 to toggle mode on/off.
2839  8CEF
2840  8CEF              ; 65339 = data read/write:
2841  8CEF              ; d0-d1 : blue intensity (last bit duplicated so Bb is Bbb)
2842  8CEF              ; d2-d4 : red intensity
2843  8CEF              ; d5-d7 : green intensity
2844  8CEF
2845  8CEF 01 3B BF     setpal ld bc,48955         ; register select.
2846  8CF2 3E 40               ld a,64             ; mode select.
2847  8CF4 ED 79               out (c),a           ; set ULAplus mode.
2848  8CF6 06 FF               ld b,255            ; data write.
2849  8CF8 3E 01               ld a,1              ; mode on.
2850  8CFA ED 79               out (c),a           ; switch on ULAplus.
2851  8CFC
2852  8CFC 06 40               ld b,64             ; number of palette table entries to write.
2853  8CFE 21 4A 86     setpa1 ld hl,palett        ; palette we want.
2854  8D01 1E 00               ld e,0              ; register number.
2855  8D03 C5           setpa0 push bc             ; store counter.
2856  8D04 06 BF               ld b,191            ; register select.
2857  8D06 7B                  ld a,e              ; register number to write.
2858  8D07 ED 79               out (c),a           ; write to port.
2859  8D09 06 FF               ld b,255            ; data select.
2860  8D0B 7E                  ld a,(hl)           ; get colour data from table.
2861  8D0C ED 79               out (c),a           ; write to port.
2862  8D0E 1C                  inc e               ; next clut entry.
2863  8D0F 23                  inc hl              ; next table entry.
2864  8D10 C1                  pop bc              ; restore counter from stack.
2865  8D11 10 F0               djnz setpa0         ; set rest of palette.
2866  8D13 C9                  ret
2867  8D14
2868  8D14              endpal equ $

2882  8D28 29                  add hl,hl

2897  8D3D FE B1               cp 177              ; is it out-of-range?
2898  8D3F D0                  ret nc              ; yes, can't colour it.
2899  8D40 07                  rlca                ; divide by 64.
2900  8D41 07                  rlca                ; quicker than 6 rrca operations.

3200  8F59

3240  8FA2 28 21               jr z,star1          ; yes, it's horizontal.
3241  8FA4 3D                  dec a               ; is it right?
3242  8FA5 28 2C               jr z,star2          ; yes, it's horizontal.
3243  8FA7 3D                  dec a               ; is it up?
3244  8FA8 28 35               jr z,star3          ; yes, it's vertical.

3248  8FAE DD 77 03     star8  ld (ix+3),a         ; set x coord.

3250  8FB4 DD 77 05     star9  ld (ix+5),a         ; set y position.
3251  8FB7 79                  ld a,c              ; direction.

3271  8FE7              ; Find particle slot for lasers or vapour trail.
3272  8FE7              ; Can't use alternate accumulator.
3273  8FE7
3274  8FE7 DD 21 B6 F9  fpslot ld ix,SHRAPN        ; shrapnel table.
3275  8FEB 11 06 00            ld de,SHRSIZ        ; size of each particle.
3276  8FEE 06 37               ld b,NUMSHR         ; number of pieces in table.
3277  8FF0 DD 7E 00     fpslt0 ld a,(ix+0)         ; get type.
3278  8FF3 17                  rla                 ; is this slot in use?
3279  8FF4 D8                  ret c               ; no, we can use it.
3280  8FF5 DD 19               add ix,de           ; point to more shrapnel.
3281  8FF7 10 F7               djnz fpslt0         ; repeat for all shrapnel.
3282  8FF9 C9                  ret                 ; out of slots, can't generate anything.
3283  8FFA
3284  8FFA              ; Create an explosion at sprite position.
3285  8FFA
3286  8FFA 4F           explod ld c,a              ; particles to create.
3287  8FFB DD E5               push ix             ; store pointer to sprite.
3288  8FFD DD 6E 08            ld l,(ix+8)         ; x coordinate.
3289  9000 DD 66 09            ld h,(ix+9)         ; y coordinate.
3290  9003 DD 21 B6 F9         ld ix,SHRAPN        ; shrapnel table.
3291  9007 11 06 00            ld de,SHRSIZ        ; size of each particle.
3292  900A 06 37               ld b,NUMSHR         ; number of pieces in table.
3293  900C DD 7E 00     expld0 ld a,(ix+0)         ; get type.
3294  900F 17                  rla                 ; is this slot in use?
3295  9010 38 07               jr c,expld1         ; no, we can use it.
3296  9012 DD 19        expld2 add ix,de           ; point to more shrapnel.
3297  9014 10 F6               djnz expld0         ; repeat for all shrapnel.
3298  9016 DD E1        expld3 pop ix              ; restore sprite pointer.
3299  9018 C9                  ret                 ; out of slots, can't generate any more.
3300  9019 79           expld1 ld a,c              ; shrapnel counter.
3301  901A E6 0F               and 15              ; 0 to 15.
3302  901C 85                  add a,l             ; add to x.
3303  901D DD 77 03            ld (ix+3),a         ; x coord.
3304  9020 3A 4C 90            ld a,(seed3)        ; crap random number.
3305  9023 E6 0F               and 15              ; 0 to 15.
3306  9025 84                  add a,h             ; add to y.
3307  9026 DD 77 05            ld (ix+5),a         ; y coord.
3308  9029 DD 36 00 02         ld (ix+0),2         ; switch it on.
3309  902D D9                  exx                 ; store coordinates.
3310  902E CD 2A 8E            call chkxy          ; plot first position.
3311  9031 CD 3F 90            call qrand          ; quick random angle.
3312  9034 E6 3C               and 60              ; keep within range.
3313  9036 DD 77 01            ld (ix+1),a         ; angle.
3314  9039 D9                  exx                 ; restore coordinates.
3315  903A 0D                  dec c               ; one less piece of shrapnel to generate.
3316  903B 20 D5               jr nz,expld2        ; back to main explosion loop.
3317  903D 18 D7               jr expld3           ; restore sprite pointer and exit.
3318  903F 3A 4C 90     qrand  ld a,(seed3)        ; random seed.
3319  9042 6F                  ld l,a              ; low byte.
3320  9043 26 00               ld h,0              ; no high byte.
3321  9045 ED 5F               ld a,r              ; r register.
3322  9047 AE                  xor (hl)            ; combine with seed.
3323  9048 32 4C 90            ld (seed3),a        ; new seed.
3324  904B C9                  ret
3325  904C 00           seed3  defb 0

3332  9056 21 C2 8D            ld hl,prosh1        ; processing routine.
3333  9059 22 B9 8D            ld (proshx+1),hl    ; modify the call.

3337  905D
3338  905D 21 B6 F9     inishr ld hl,SHRAPN        ; table.
3339  9060 06 37               ld b,NUMSHR         ; shrapnel pieces to process.
3340  9062 11 06 00            ld de,SHRSIZ        ; distance to next.
3341  9065 36 FF        inish0 ld (hl),255         ; kill the shrapnel.

3343  9068 10 FB               djnz inish0         ; round again.

3346  906B              ; Check for collision between laser and sprite.
3347  906B
3348  906B 21 B6 F9     lcol   ld hl,SHRAPN        ; shrapnel table.
3349  906E 11 06 00            ld de,SHRSIZ        ; size of each particle.
3350  9071 06 37               ld b,NUMSHR         ; number of pieces in table.
3351  9073 7E           lcol0  ld a,(hl)           ; get type.
3352  9074 A7                  and a               ; is this slot a laser?
3353  9075 28 04               jr z,lcol1          ; yes, check collision.
3354  9077 19           lcol3  add hl,de           ; point to more shrapnel.
3355  9078 10 F9               djnz lcol0          ; repeat for all shrapnel.
3356  907A C9                  ret                 ; no collision, carry not set.
3357  907B E5           lcol1  push hl             ; store pointer to laser.
3358  907C 23                  inc hl              ; direction.
3359  907D 23                  inc hl              ; not used.
3360  907E 23                  inc hl              ; x position.
3361  907F 7E                  ld a,(hl)           ; get x.
3362  9080 DD 96 08            sub (ix+X)          ; subtract sprite x.
3363  9083 FE 10        lcolh  cp 16               ; within range?
3364  9085 30 0A               jr nc,lcol2         ; no, missed.
3365  9087 23                  inc hl              ; not used.
3366  9088 23                  inc hl              ; y position.
3367  9089 7E                  ld a,(hl)           ; get y.
3368  908A DD 96 09            sub (ix+Y)          ; subtract sprite y.
3369  908D FE 10               cp 16               ; within range?
3370  908F 38 03               jr c,lcol4          ; yes, collision occurred.
3371  9091 E1           lcol2  pop hl              ; restore laser pointer from stack.
3372  9092 18 E3               jr lcol3
3373  9094 E1           lcol4  pop hl              ; restore laser pointer.
3374  9095 C9                  ret                 ; return with carry set for collision.

3545  91D0 11 0A 00     numdg2 ld de,10            ; tens column.
3546  91D3 CD DB 91            call numdg          ; show digit.
3547  91D6 F6 10               or 16               ; last digit is always shown.
3548  91D8 11 01 00            ld de,1             ; units column.
3549  91DB E6 30        numdg  and 48              ; clear carry, clear digit.
3550  91DD ED 52        numdg1 sbc hl,de           ; subtract from column.
3551  91DF 38 05               jr c,numdg0         ; nothing to show.
3552  91E1 F6 10               or 16               ; something to show, make it a digit.
3553  91E3 3C                  inc a               ; increment digit.
3554  91E4 18 F7               jr numdg1           ; repeat until column is zero.
3555  91E6 19           numdg0 add hl,de           ; restore total.
3556  91E7 FE 20               cp 32               ; leading space?
3557  91E9 C8                  ret z               ; yes, don't write that.
3558  91EA 02                  ld (bc),a           ; write digit to buffer.
3559  91EB 03                  inc bc              ; next buffer position.
3560  91EC C9                  ret
3561  91ED 6F           num2dd ld l,a              ; put accumulator in l.
3562  91EE 26 00               ld h,0              ; blank high byte of hl.
3563  91F0 3E 20               ld a,32             ; leading spaces.
3564  91F2 11 64 00            ld de,100           ; hundreds column.
3565  91F5 CD DB 91            call numdg          ; show digit.
3566  91F8 F6 10               or 16               ; second digit is always shown.
3567  91FA 18 D4               jr numdg2
3568  91FC 6F           num2td ld l,a              ; put accumulator in l.
3569  91FD 26 00               ld h,0              ; blank high byte of hl.
3570  91FF 3E 30               ld a,48             ; leading spaces.
3571  9201 18 C7               jr numdg3
3572  9203
3573  9203 06 06        inisc  ld b,6              ; digits to initialise.
3574  9205 36 30        inisc0 ld (hl),'0'         ; write zero digit.
3575  9207 23                  inc hl              ; next column.
3576  9208 10 FB               djnz inisc0         ; repeat for all digits.

3579  920B
3580  920B              ; Multiply h by d and return in hl.
3581  920B
3582  920B 5A           imul   ld e,d              ; HL = H * D
3583  920C 4C                  ld c,h              ; make c first multiplier.
3584  920D 21 00 00     imul0  ld hl,0             ; zeroise total.
3585  9210 54                  ld d,h              ; zeroise high byte.
3586  9211 06 08               ld b,8              ; repeat 8 times.
3587  9213 CB 19        imul1  rr c                ; rotate rightmost bit into carry.
3588  9215 30 02               jr nc,imul2         ; wasn't set.
3589  9217 19                  add hl,de           ; bit was set, so add de.
3590  9218 A7                  and a               ; reset carry.
3591  9219 CB 13        imul2  rl e                ; shift de 1 bit left.
3592  921B CB 12               rl d
3593  921D 10 F4               djnz imul1          ; repeat 8 times.
3594  921F C9                  ret
3595  9220
3596  9220              ; Divide d by e and return in d, remainder in a.
3597  9220
3598  9220 AF           idiv   xor a
3599  9221 06 08               ld b,8              ; bits to shift.
3600  9223 CB 22        idiv0  sla d               ; multiply d by 2.
3601  9225 17                  rla                 ; shift carry into remainder.
3602  9226 BB                  cp e                ; test if e is smaller.
3603  9227 38 02               jr c,idiv1          ; e is greater, no division this time.
3604  9229 93                  sub e               ; subtract it.
3605  922A 14                  inc d               ; rotate into d.
3606  922B 10 F6        idiv1  djnz idiv0
3607  922D C9                  ret

3640  9259              ; Write the contents of our AY buffer to the AY registers.
3641  9259
3642  9259 21 71 92     w8912  ld hl,snddat        ; start of AY-3-8912 register data.
3643  925C 11 00 0E            ld de,14*256        ; start with register 0, 14 to write.
3644  925F 0E FD               ld c,253            ; low byte of port to write.
3645  9261 06 FF        w8912a ld b,255            ; port 65533=select soundchip register.
3646  9263 ED 59               out (c),e           ; tell chip which register we're writing.
3647  9265 7E                  ld a,(hl)           ; value to write.
3648  9266 06 BF               ld b,191            ; port 49149=write value to register.
3649  9268 ED 79               out (c),a           ; this is what we're putting there.
3650  926A 1C                  inc e               ; next sound chip register.
3651  926B 23                  inc hl              ; next byte to write.
3652  926C 15                  dec d               ; decrement loop counter.
3653  926D C2 61 92            jp nz,w8912a        ; repeat until done.
3654  9270 C9                  ret
3655  9271
3656  9271 00 00        snddat defw 0              ; tone registers, channel A.
3657  9273 00 00               defw 0              ; channel B tone registers.
3658  9275 00 00               defw 0              ; as above, channel C.
3659  9277 00           sndwnp defb 0              ; white noise period.
3660  9278 3C           sndmix defb 60             ; tone/noise mixer control.
3661  9279 00           sndv1  defb 0              ; channel A amplitude/envelope generator.
3662  927A 00           sndv2  defb 0              ; channel B amplitude/envelope.
3663  927B 00           sndv3  defb 0              ; channel C amplitude/envelope.
3664  927C 00 00               defw 0              ; duration of each note.
3665  927E 00                  defb 0

3669  9282 C0                  ret nz              ; we're not.
3670  9283 7E                  ld a,(hl)           ; fetch byte.

4852  9906 0D                  dec c               ; loop counter.

5596  9D84              ; Gravity processing.
5597  9D84
5598  9D84 DD 7E 0D     grav   ld a,(ix+13)        ; in-air flag.
5599  9D87 A7                  and a               ; are we in the air?
5600  9D88 C8                  ret z               ; no we are not.
5601  9D89 3C                  inc a               ; increment it.
5602  9D8A CA D4 9D            jp z,ogrv           ; set to 255, use old gravity.
5603  9D8D DD 77 0D            ld (ix+13),a        ; write new setting.
5604  9D90 1F                  rra                 ; every other frame.
5605  9D91 30 0A               jr nc,grav0         ; don't apply gravity this time.
5606  9D93 DD 7E 0E            ld a,(ix+14)        ; pixels to move.
5607  9D96 FE 10               cp 16               ; reached maximum?
5608  9D98 28 03               jr z,grav0          ; yes, continue.
5609  9D9A DD 34 0E            inc (ix+14)         ; slow down ascent/speed up fall.
5610  9D9D DD 7E 0E     grav0  ld a,(ix+14)        ; get distance to move.
5611  9DA0 CB 2F               sra a               ; divide by 2.
5612  9DA2 A7                  and a               ; any movement required?
5613  9DA3 C8                  ret z               ; no, not this time.
5614  9DA4 FE 80               cp 128              ; is it up or down?
5615  9DA6 30 0C               jr nc,gravu         ; it's up.
5616  9DA8 47           gravd  ld b,a              ; set pixels to move.
5617  9DA9 CD 77 97     gravd0 call cangd          ; can we go down?
5618  9DAC 20 15               jr nz,gravst        ; can't move down, so stop.
5619  9DAE DD 34 08            inc (ix+8)          ; adjust new x coord.
5620  9DB1 10 F6               djnz gravd0
5621  9DB3 C9                  ret
5622  9DB4 ED 44        gravu  neg                 ; flip the sign so it's positive.
5623  9DB6 47                  ld b,a              ; set pixels to move.
5624  9DB7 CD 54 97     gravu0 call cangu          ; can we go up?
5625  9DBA C2 5B 9E            jp nz,ifalls        ; can't move up, go down next.
5626  9DBD DD 35 08            dec (ix+8)          ; adjust new x coord.
5627  9DC0 10 F5               djnz gravu0
5628  9DC2 C9                  ret
5629  9DC3 DD 7E 0E     gravst ld a,(ix+14)        ; jump pointer high.
5630  9DC6 DD 36 0D 00         ld (ix+13),0        ; reset falling flag.
5631  9DCA DD 36 0E 00         ld (ix+14),0        ; store new speed.
5632  9DCE FE 08               cp 8                ; was speed the maximum?
5633  9DD0 CA E1 72     evftf  jp z,evnt15         ; yes, fallen too far.
5634  9DD3 C9                  ret

5889  9F96              ; Ticker routine is called 25 times per second.

5900  9FA3 CB 16        scrly0 rl (hl)             ; rotate left.

5915  9FBB 07                  rlca                ; multiply by 8 to find char.

5918  9FBF 57                  ld d,a              ; that's our high byte.
5919  9FC0 78                  ld a,b              ; restore the shift.
